# Specification: Schema versioning

## 1. Business Goal

<!-- last written by: spec-author, 2026-10-14 (pin command name and placement) -->

A repository's `.spec/schema/` is a copy of the consumer's from one day, and nothing says which day, so either every repository moves with a consumer repository at once or the copies diverge silently, and a repository that lags cannot catch up on purpose; and a bare version number cannot tell a breaking schema change from a harmless one, so every change forces every consumer into a deliberate upgrade and the owner cannot try one on this repository alone. This Feature removes that failure state: the manifest pins one `major.minor.patch` `schemaVersion`, numbered by whether a tree's verdict can change; the tool embeds the versions it ships - every published one from `1.0.0` on - and checks with exactly the pinned one; `specht schema pin <version>` moves the pin up or down, and `specht upgrade` brings the schema set and the templates to the pin and prints what changed. Lagging becomes deliberate, moving becomes one command each way, the number says whether a move can fail the build, and a document is only ever changed by the agent, from the report.

## 2. User Needs

<!-- last written by: spec-author, 2026-10-28 (A-3 amended, OQ-24) -->

| #   | Persona                                             | Need                                                                                                 | Pain Point Today                                                                                                                                          |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | The maintainer of a lagging repository              | Pin a version and stay there until ready; the newest tool still checks it                            | A fork from one day, with no version to name                                                                                                              |
| 2   | The maintainer moving a repository                  | One command that brings the schema set and templates to the pinned version and says what changed     | Hand-copy, then diff by eye                                                                                                                               |
| 3   | The agent migrating documents                       | After an upgrade, the check reports what each document is missing, with what the rule expected       | Nothing says what changed between versions                                                                                                                |
| 4   | A maintainer trying a schema edit locally           | Point the check at the on-disk schema files instead of the embedded ones, deliberately               | The on-disk copy is the only copy, so a hand edit is silently authoritative                                                                               |
| 5   | `0001-F4`                                           | The embedded version set and the newest version number to write                                      | N/A (internal dependency)                                                                                                                                 |
| 6   | `0001-F3`                                           | The version and the source to name in the report                                                     | N/A (internal dependency)                                                                                                                                 |
| 7   | The owner trying a schema change on this repository | A version number that says whether a change can fail a tree, and a pin that moves up or down         | An integer version cannot tell a breaking change from a non-breaking one, so every change forces every consumer into a deliberate upgrade (decision 0005) |
| 8   | A maintainer with a template of its own             | Keep that template across `upgrade`, and still get the pinned version's copy of every other template | `upgrade` rewrites every template with the pinned version's; the frontmatter schemas have an opt-out (B-029) and the templates have none (decision 0006)  |
| 9   | The owner changing a shipped file                   | Know which version is still open to change, and start the next one with one command                  | The folders are `v1` and state no version, nothing marks one published, and a new one is a hand copy (decision 0009)                                      |

### Assumptions

| ID  | Assumption                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A-1 | A manifest with no `schemaVersion` key is `0.1.0`, the version that predates the key. the baseline manifest at the baseline commit has no key and must keep checking unchanged (Should-7). Amended 2026-10-09 (decision 0005): was version 1.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| A-2 | `0.1.0` is the schema set the first published package embeds (decision 0002, decision 0005): `0001-F1` A-3's set, plus the manifest keys `0001-F5` and `0001-F6` add, plus the optional `title` and `description` keys decision 0003 adds to the epic frontmatter schema, plus `0001-F5` decision 0004's loosening of a Feature's `epic` and a task's `parent` and `id`, since nothing is published before brief § 8 step 5. Amended 2026-10-09 (decision 0005): was version 1. Amended 2026-10-11 (decision 0007): the first publish no longer waits for brief § 8 step 5, so `0.1.0` is the set as it stands when the first tag is pushed; which of the parts listed here it then holds is OQ-30. |
| A-3 | "Prints what changed" is the name of every file rewritten and the pinned version, and no diff (OQ-3, resolved 2026-10-08); a manifest `upgrade` adds a key to is a file rewritten (OQ-24, resolved 2026-10-28). Provisional on OQ-21: decision 0005 removed the move the owner's version pair described, and whether `upgrade` also names the version the files were at is not decided.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| A-4 | The version lock is one committed file in this repository that records each locked schema version with one digest (decision 0009). A version it records is locked; the one it does not is the working version, open to change. A version is locked before it is published (`0055-F6` B-013), so every published version is locked. Its path and format are § 7's (OQ-52).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

## 4. Constraints

<!-- last written by: spec-author, 2026-10-29 (C-17 amended, OQ-55) -->

| ID   | Constraint                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Rules Out                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| C-1  | A schema version, once published in a package, is never changed in place; from `1.0.0` on it is embedded in every later tool and never deleted, and a later tool may stop embedding a published `0.x.y` (decision 0002; amended 2026-10-09, decision 0005; amended 2026-10-11, decision 0007, resolving OQ-10: was every published version embedded for good, with `0.x` open).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Patching a published version in place, a `0.x.y` included; dropping a published `1.0.0` or higher "nobody uses it"; counting an unpublished step-3 pack as shipped; a freeze that starts when the `specht.tool` package reaches `1.0.0` (C-12).                                                                                                              |
| C-2  | `upgrade` writes only `<root>/.specht/manifest.json` and under `<root>/.specht/schema/frontmatter/` and `<root>/.specht/templates/` (amended 2026-10-11, `0001-F5` decision 0011: was under `<root>/.specht/schema/` and the templates folder), and with the on-disk source selected and no upstream source recorded it writes no frontmatter schema (B-029; amended 2026-10-08, OQ-8); it writes no template the manifest names as the consumer's, and touches no file under that templates folder the pinned version does not ship (B-060, B-063; amended 2026-10-09, decision 0006).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Rewriting a specification, an epic, a record or a `.feature`; `--fix`; overwriting a consumer's own frontmatter schemas; overwriting a template the consumer has named as its own; deleting or tidying a template file the tool never shipped.                                                                                                               |
| C-3  | Retired 2026-10-09 (decision 0005): `upgrade` moves one version at a time.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Nothing: the owner dropped it. The pin command moves to any shipped version (B-043, B-044), and `upgrade` brings the files to the pin (B-005).                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| C-4  | The consumer's manifest is always read from `<root>/.specht/manifest.json`; the pin selects the frontmatter schemas, rule vocabulary and manifest interpretation, and omitted settings are filled from that version's shipped defaults without replacing explicit consumer settings (`0001-F5` B-048, B-049; owner, 2026-10-13; `0001-F5` decision 0010). Only the frontmatter schemas have a selectable source.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | An embedded manifest replacing a missing consumer file; defaults winning over explicit sections, grammars, roles or rule settings; newest-version interpretation applied to an older pin; a schema-source switch also switching the manifest.                                                                                                                |
| C-5  | The tool, not the consumer, knows the difference between two versions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | A migration script shipped to the consumer; a consumer-side changelog the tool reads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| C-6  | The `$id` of each frontmatter schema in specht's own embedded set is `https://github.com/rlittlesii/specht/schema/`, then its version segment, `v<major.minor.patch>`, the folder's name (B-086, B-087), then `/frontmatter/` and its file name, as the file sits under `.specht/` (amended 2026-10-11, `0001-F5` decision 0011; the mirrored path is derived by spec-author, not stated by the owner: a default spec-author proposed, contained in a plan the owner approved on 2026-10-11); a consumer's upstream schema carries its own `$id` (decision 0004; amended 2026-10-08, OQ-7). Amended 2026-10-11 (decision 0009, resolving OQ-22): the segment's form was provisional, the integer `v<n>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | A version-less `$id`; an integer or ordinal segment; a bare `0.1.0` segment with no `v`; an `$id` under another repository for specht's set; a consumer's schema forced under `rlittlesii/specht`; an `$id` whose path past the version differs from the file's path under `.specht/schema/`.                                                                |
| C-7  | Every path `upgrade` derives and prints is relative to the root with `/` separators; a path the user typed (`--root`) is echoed as given.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | An absolute path for a rewritten file; a `--root` argument rewritten before it is echoed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| C-8  | `init` and `upgrade` are the only commands that may make a network call, and only to fetch an upstream schema source the manifest records or the same init run will record - its frontmatter schemas and the templates it hashes (0001-F4 decision 0003, ratified 2026-10-12; amended 2026-10-24, decision 0016: was its frontmatter schemas only); the check never makes one (decision 0004).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | A fetch, a cached remote read or a version lookup at check time; a network call without a recorded source or a source the same init run will record; a template fetched without a hash recorded for it.                                                                                                                                                      |
| C-9  | An upstream schema source is recorded with its version and one content hash for each fetched file, a template included, and a fetched file is written only when its content matches its hash (decision 0004). Amended 2026-10-11 (owner; decision 0008, ratification): was a content hash. Amended 2026-10-24 (decision 0016): a template the record hashes is a fetched file.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | An unpinned or "latest" source; content written before its hash is checked; one hash over the whole set; one hash over the templates.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| C-10 | An upstream schema source's version and content hashes are independent of `schemaVersion`: `schemaVersion` pins the rule vocabulary and the templates the manifest neither names as the consumer's (amended 2026-10-09, decision 0006) nor the record hashes, and the recorded upstream version pins the frontmatter schemas and the templates the record hashes (amended 2026-10-24, decision 0016: was the frontmatter schemas only); only the consumer moves the upstream version, by editing the manifest (owner, 2026-10-08; decision 0004).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `upgrade` moving the upstream version; an upstream version derived from `schemaVersion`, or the reverse; `upgrade` rewriting a template the record hashes with the pinned version's.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| C-11 | A schema version is numbered by its verdict: a patch changes no tree's verdict; from `1.0.0` on a minor only accepts more, so no tree that passed fails, and any change under which a tree that passed can fail - a new `SPEC###` included - is a major; before `1.0.0` a `0.minor` step may fail a tree that passed, a new `SPEC###` included (decision 0005; amended 2026-10-11, decision 0007: was one rule for every version).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | From `1.0.0` on, a new rule or a stricter schema in a minor; at any version, a new rule or a stricter schema in a patch, and a patch that changes any verdict; numbering by the size of the change, by date or by tool release.                                                                                                                              |
| C-12 | A schema version is numbered independently of the `specht.tool` package version; neither is derived from the other (decision 0005).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Reading the schema version from `version.json` or the assembly; a tool release that must bump the schema version; a schema version that must bump the tool's version.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| C-13 | Once a manifest exists, its `schemaVersion` changes only through `specht schema pin` or a hand edit, and `specht schema pin` writes nothing else (decision 0005).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | A pin that also rewrites the schemas or the templates; an `upgrade` that moves the pin; `init` rewriting an existing manifest's pin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| C-14 | A template is the consumer's only because the manifest names it (derived from OQ-25 and OQ-8, not stated by the owner; decision 0006), and a recorded upstream source neither makes a template the consumer's nor writes one the manifest names (owner, 2026-10-24; amended 2026-10-24, decision 0016, resolving OQ-26: was a reading that did not prejudge the upstream template source).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Ownership inferred from the on-disk schema source, from a hand edit or a difference in content, from the file being one the tool does not ship, or from a hash the record holds; an all-or-nothing switch over the templates folder; an upstream template written over one the manifest names.                                                               |
| C-15 | The first release fetches from a consumer's own upstream source on any `https` host without authentication: the tool reads no credential and sends none with a fetch, and an authenticated source is the next release's (owner, 2026-10-24: "Private sources should be allowed in the first release." and "Assume the private source has no authentication for this version. We can do authenticated sources NEXT version."; decision 0016, resolving OQ-41). Amended 2026-10-24 (decision 0016): was public sources only (owner, 2026-10-11: "public sources for v1. for v2 we should offer pats in the keychain or os equivalent"; decision 0008, ratification), with "the first release" for "v1" spec-author's reading.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | A token read from the environment, `SPECHT_SCHEMA_TOKEN` included; a token in the manifest or in an argument; a read of a credential store; answering a credential challenge; refusing a source because its host is not specht's.                                                                                                                            |
| C-16 | The name of a version's folder is the one statement of which version its files are: the same name in every tree a version's files sit in (B-086) and in the embedded resource names (owner, 2026-10-11; decision 0009).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | A mapping from a folder to its version kept in code; a folder named by an integer or an ordinal; trees that name one version differently; ordering versions by folder name as text, so `v0.10.0` before `v0.9.0`.                                                                                                                                            |
| C-17 | The version lock records one digest for each locked version and no list of its files, and a digest it records is never rewritten once its version is published: a changed file under a published version is a new version. A locked version no published package has carried may be relocked, and schema `0.2.0` is relocked when the nine rules B-115 adds land in it (B-013; owner, 2026-10-11; decision 0009; one digest and no file list is the approved plan's; amended 2026-10-14, decision 0014, resolving OQ-54: was a digest never rewritten and a changed file under any locked version a new version). How a relock is made - the unpublished version's entry removed by hand in a reviewed change, then recorded again by the lock target (B-092) - is spec-author's reading, not stated by the owner (decision 0014). Amended 2026-10-29 (owner, resolving OQ-55): a version's lock entry stays when a later tool stops shipping it. | Locking a published version again so a changed version passes; a published version's lock entry edited by hand; a lock target that rewrites an entry it finds (B-093); a file list in the lock, the index `0001-F3` § 5 row 10 and `0001-F10` C-2 keep out; a freeze held by review alone; removing the lock entry of a version a later tool stops shipping. |

## 5. Out of Scope

<!-- last written by: spec-author, 2026-10-29 (rows 31 and 32 added, decision 0019) -->

| #   | Item                                                                                                                      | Exclusion Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | Migrating documents between versions                                                                                      | brief § 2; the agent, from the violations and their `expected` (`0001-F3`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| 2   | Withdrawn 2026-10-09: a `downgrade` command                                                                               | The owner dropped the boundary (decision 0005): the pin command moves the pin down as well as up (B-044), and `upgrade` brings the files to it (B-005).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 3   | The content of any schema version after `0.1.0`                                                                           | Its own epic, after this Feature; nothing here says what changes. Its first content is epic `0101`, the linting rules (`0101-F1` decision 0001); the number it takes is OQ-18.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 4   | Versioning the report document                                                                                            | `0001-F3` C-4; the report is versioned with the tool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 5   | A version pinned per specification                                                                                        | Decided: the version is the repository's (brief § 6); a document declares none.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| 6   | `init` fetching a recorded upstream schema source                                                                         | `0001-F4` B-014 to B-016 and B-027, under C-8 and C-9 (corrected 2026-10-11: was B-014 and B-015).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 7   | Epics opt-in as a schema version after `0.1.0`                                                                            | Additive to `0.1.0`, which is unpublished (decision 0002; `0001-F5` decision 0004). Amended 2026-10-09 (decision 0005): was version 2, additive to version 1.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| 8   | Pin ranges other than `latest-patch` and `latest-minor` - a caret or tilde range, a wildcard, a minimum, a `latest-major` | Decision 0005 names two policies; a wider float lets a tool release fail a tree that passed (C-11). B-056.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| 9   | A pin that floats by default                                                                                              | Decision 0005: a pin is exact unless a pin policy says otherwise (B-055).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| 10  | A schema version derived from, or moving with, the `specht.tool` package version                                          | C-12.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 11  | `specht schema pin` rewriting the schema set or the templates                                                             | C-13; bringing the files to the pin is `upgrade`'s (B-005, B-046).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 12  | `upgrade` moving the pin, or taking a target version                                                                      | C-13; B-052.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| 13  | Hosting or fetching specht's own schema versions from the web                                                             | Open (OQ-9). Until it is answered every version the tool ships is embedded (C-1; amended 2026-10-11, decision 0007: was every version), and only an upstream source the manifest records is fetched (decision 0004).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 14  | Withdrawn 2026-10-24: an upstream template source, templates fetched from a source the manifest records                   | In scope (decision 0016, resolving OQ-26): the template source extends the `spechtSchema` record (B-125 to B-137). Was: decided by the owner as a later stage (decision 0006), its shape open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 15  | Validating the content of a template, the consumer's or the tool's                                                        | The check reads no template (B-065); a template is a blank the agent copies (`0001-F4` § 5 row 4).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 16  | Naming template ownership, or a template source, in the report                                                            | `0001-F3` B-005 names the schema version and the schema source; the check reads no template (B-065).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 17  | A new schema version for the key that names the consumer's templates                                                      | It is part of `0.1.0`, which is unpublished (decision 0002; decision 0006).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| 18  | `init` treating a template the manifest names differently                                                                 | `0001-F4`: `init` writes each absent file of the parts it is asked for and overwrites none, whatever the manifest names (decision 0006; `0001-F4` decision 0004).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 19  | A root with no manifest coming to record an upstream schema source                                                        | `0001-F4` (OQ-6): `init` is the command such a root runs. Ratified 2026-10-12: `0001-F4` decision 0003; B-035 to B-040.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 20  | A credential for a source that asks for one                                                                               | The owner, 2026-10-24: "We can do authenticated sources NEXT version." Its mechanism - the keychain of the owner's 2026-10-11 direction ("pats in the keychain or os equivalent"), the environment or a CI secret - is not specified. Decided for then: sent only to the host of the recorded `location`, over `https`, and never carried by a redirect to another host (decision 0016 call 2; OQ-41). Until then the tool reads no credential (C-15; B-083; B-137). Amended 2026-10-24 (decision 0016). |
| 21  | A package as an upstream schema source                                                                                    | Decided (decision 0004) and not built for the first release (owner, 2026-10-11; decision 0008, ratification): a recorded `location` is an `https` address (B-070).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 22  | A version stated inside a template                                                                                        | A template is copied into a consumer's tree and into every specification written from it, so a stamp travels and goes stale; the folder states the version (C-16). The plan the owner approved, 2026-10-11 (decision 0009).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| 23  | A version number of their own for the templates or for the rule pages                                                     | Rejected by the owner (decision 0009): it needs three pins or a compatibility table. One number covers the set (B-013); C-10 and `0001-F3` C-6 stand.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 24  | A version metadata file in each version's folder                                                                          | Rejected by the owner with OQ-22's reading (c) (decision 0009): the folder's name states the version (C-16).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| 25  | Writing a cut version's rule vocabulary: the engine's list of the rule ids a version holds stays written by hand          | ADR-0010, open point 7, owns the vocabulary being written twice. The cut writes only the version's folders (B-100).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| 26  | A delta between version folders, or a fallback to an older version's folder for a file a version lacks                    | Each version's folders are a whole snapshot (B-096, B-099). The cost, a tool carrying each version's files, is the one decision 0007 accepted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 27  | `SPEC060` scoped per claim: a § 9 `Missing` row reported only once the item that delivers that claim is done              | Not built (decision 0015 call 9): the consumer item schema carries no claim ids, so the rule keys on the Feature's delivery status (B-118). OQ-59.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 28  | The check verifying a fetched template against its hash, or stopping when one is absent                                   | The check reads no template (B-065; row 15), so B-077 and B-080 have no template counterpart. Spec-author's reading (decision 0016 call 9); the owner did not speak to the check.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 29  | A template source recorded apart from `spechtSchema`, or one record per template                                          | Considered by the owner and not taken (decision 0016): one record holds the set (B-125).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| 30  | An upstream template replacing a template the manifest names as the consumer's                                            | Considered by the owner and not taken (decision 0016): a named template is never written, whatever source is recorded (B-135; C-14).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 31  | `upgrade` taking `--schema-source`                                                                                        | The owner, 2026-10-29 (OQ-1; decision 0019 call 2): only the manifest's `schemaSource` selects the source `upgrade` reads. B-009 names the option on the check alone.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 32  | Rejecting, or warning on, a `consumerTemplates` name the pinned version does not ship                                     | The owner, 2026-10-29 (OQ-27 (b); decision 0019 call 4): the name is accepted and has no effect (B-144).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

## 6. Concern Separation

<!-- last written by: implementer, 2026-10-14 -->

Item `0042` built the version set and the pin as an integer; item `0120` converts both to `major.minor.patch` (decision 0005). The schema source (`0043`, built 2026-10-14), the frozen-version proof (`0044`), `upgrade` (`0045`-`0048`), the pin and list commands (`0121` to `0123`, `0125`, built 2026-10-14) and the pin policy (`0124`) extend this table. Item `0176` adds the last five rows, on the upstream schema source the manifest records (decision 0008), built 2026-10-11.

| Concern                                                                                                                             | Classification | Where                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| What a schema version is: three numbers, ordered by major, minor, then patch (decision 0005)                                        | Business       | `SemanticVersion`                                                                                                                                                                     |
| A manifest pins one version; no key is `0.1.0` (B-002)                                                                              | Business       | `SpecManifestParser.Parse`, onto `SpecStructure.SchemaVersion`                                                                                                                        |
| A `schemaVersion` that is not a `major.minor.patch` string is a rejected manifest naming the value (B-039)                          | Business       | `SpecManifestParser.Parse` with `SemanticVersion.TryParse`, throwing `SpechtManifestException`; `CheckCommand`'s existing catch folds it to stderr and exit `3`                       |
| Which versions the tool ships, each named `major.minor.patch` (B-004, C-1)                                                          | Business       | `SpechtResources` reads raw texts; an injected `SchemaVersions` assembles the shipped versions                                                                                        |
| Which version an embedded folder holds                                                                                              | Technical      | `SchemaVersions.ReadVersions`, parsing the folder's name through `SemanticVersion.TryParse` (B-086, C-16)                                                                             |
| An unshipped pin is a rejected manifest naming the pin and the shipped set (B-003)                                                  | Business       | `SpecManifestParser.PinnedVersion`, throwing `SpechtManifestException`; the same catch and exit `3`                                                                                   |
| Init writes the manifest of the highest shipping folder by semver precedence (B-012)                                                | Both           | `InitWriter.Newest`, selecting through `SemanticVersion` and preserving the selected manifest bytes                                                                                   |
| A pin is exact: a later patch or minor is never selected (B-055)                                                                    | Business       | `SchemaVersions.Find`, by equality of the three numbers                                                                                                                               |
| The pinned version's frontmatter schemas validate the tree; the manifest stays on disk (B-001, C-4)                                 | Both           | `SpecSchemasLoader.Load`: the manifest from `<root>/.specht/manifest.json`, the three frontmatter schemas from the selected version                                                   |
| Each version's rule vocabulary (B-014, brief § 4)                                                                                   | Business       | `SchemaVersions.Shipped`, hand-written per `SemanticVersion`                                                                                                                          |
| Only the pinned vocabulary is evaluated, counted and reported (B-014)                                                               | Business       | `SpechtRunner.Evaluate(SpecModel, IEnumerable<ISpecRule>)`, over `model.Schemas.Version.RuleIds` (item `0105`, ADR-0008; `0001-F1` § 7)                                               |
| Version `0.1.0`'s epic `title` and `description` (B-022, B-023, decision 0003)                                                      | Business       | `src/tool/schema/v0.1.0/frontmatter/epic.schema.json`, embedded into the engine; `FrontmatterSchemaRule` already maps an epic failure to `SPEC004`                                    |
| The report names the version and the embedded source (B-002, `0001-F3` B-005)                                                       | Business       | `SpechtReport.SchemaVersion`; `SpecReportDocument.From` writes its text                                                                                                               |
| Embedding the shipping copy into the engine without a second copy                                                                   | Technical      | `src/specht/specht.csproj` links `src/tool/schema/v*/frontmatter/*.schema.json` as `EmbeddedResource`                                                                                 |
| A manifest records an upstream schema source in `spechtSchema`; no key records none (B-068)                                         | Business       | `SpecManifestParser.Parse`, onto `SpecStructure.SpechtSchema`: an `UpstreamSchemaSource`, or null                                                                                     |
| A record that lacks a member, or holds a `location`, a `version` or a hash in another form, is a rejected manifest (B-069 to B-073) | Business       | `SpecManifestParser.Parse`, its faults joined to the ones `Check` collects, throwing `SpechtManifestException`; `CheckCommand`'s existing catch and exit `3` (`0001-F5` B-022, B-023) |
| Which kinds are fetched: Feature and item, and epic when the manifest declares the epic grammar (B-075, B-076)                      | Business       | `UpstreamSchemaSource.Files`, one `UpstreamSchemaFile` for each fetched kind, built from the manifest's own `identifiers.epic` before the default fills it                            |
| A fetched kind's address: `location` followed by its `frontmatterSchemas` file name (B-074)                                         | Business       | `UpstreamSchemaFile.Address`, written once at load                                                                                                                                    |
| Where a rejection of the record is written                                                                                          | Technical      | Code in `SpecManifestParser`: the manifest file is data, and no JSON Schema describes it                                                                                              |
| Recording the newest shipped version once (B-092, B-093, C-17)                                                                      | Both           | `SchemaVersionLock.LockNewestAsync`, called by the `LockSchemaVersion` build target                                                                                                   |
| One SHA-256 over a version's ordered paths and bytes (B-089)                                                                        | Technical      | `SchemaVersionLock.ComputeDigestAsync`, over the schema, manifest, template and rule-page trees                                                                                       |

| Reading a recorded source's local copy and checking its hashes (B-024, B-077 to B-081) | Both | `SpecManifestStore.ReadFrontmatterSchema` reads unchanged bytes; `UpstreamSchemaCopy` verifies them without I/O; `SpecSchemasLoader` builds the schemas |
| Keeping the pinned vocabulary and reporting the source (B-028, B-032) | Business | `SpecSchemas.Version` keeps the pin and its rule ids; `SpecSchemas.Source` flows through the runner and report document |
| The check makes no network call (B-025, C-8) | Technical | The store reads local files only; the verifier takes bytes and the loader uses no network type |

| Concern                                                                         | Kind      | Boundary                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Explicit selection and CLI precedence (B-009, B-101 to B-103)                   | Business  | A nullable typed selection carried per run; the manifest parser retains omission and the schema loader resolves it                     |
| Recorded-source integrity under explicit selection (B-036, B-024, B-080)        | Business  | Manifest contradiction validation and the existing local-copy verifier before schema compilation                                       |
| Required disk kinds, file names and controlled failures (B-009, B-104 to B-107) | Both      | The parsed declaration selects kinds; `SpecManifestStore` reads bytes and the loader names relative file failures                      |
| Report provenance (B-011)                                                       | Technical | The existing `SpecSchemaSource` value flows through the runner and report mapper; input selection and resolved provenance are distinct |
| References deferred by the schema library (B-105 to B-107)                      | Both      | The existing frontmatter evaluation boundary catches unresolved local references with their root-relative schema file identity         |

## 7. Technical Design

### Schema command boundary

<!-- last written by: implementer, 2026-10-14 -->

`SchemaVersionManager` in `src/specht/Manifest/SchemaVersionManager.cs` lists,
prepares selector choices and pins through the existing manifest store, parser,
editor and catalog. The host calls this concrete service directly and owns
presentation and keyboard interaction. Listing reads no schema or specification
files; an absent manifest has no pin, and a missing version key means `0.1.0`.

The existing `Program.cs` composition is retained. Host and command tests use
the schema slice's same command registration and the engine's registration.
ADR-0014 records the owner's benchmark gate and source-generated Mediator
evaluation; no CQRS contracts, dispatcher, model-reader migration or global
host-factory migration is included here. Item `0216` owns that evidence, and
the existing architecture migration items remain gated.

`ManifestPinEditor` validates under the usable current pin, or newest for repair,
and changes only the root JSON value token. It decodes UTF-8 and BOM-marked
UTF-16/UTF-32 in either byte order, as the existing check does. UTF-8 parser token
positions are mapped through character offsets to the original encoding's byte
offsets; only the changed token or missing-key insertion uses that encoding.
BOM, whitespace, line endings, nested values and all other bytes survive.
`SpecManifestStore` stages a complete sibling file and atomically replaces the
manifest, cleaning the staging file on failure. No schema, template or document
is read or rewritten.

Choices undergo the same root and non-version settings validation before the
host offers the Spectre.Console selector. Arrow keys choose and Enter confirms;
pin then revalidates before writing. No terminal means exit `2` with an explicit
version hint. Explicit arguments skip the prompt. Decision 0012 retains input
precedence, repair and downgrade preservation.

### Explicit frontmatter schema selection

**Implemented 2026-10-14 (items `0043` and `0177`).**
[Decision 0011](decisions/0011-explicit-schema-selection-is-versioned-and-command-line-first.md)
owns the public contract. The source switch changes only frontmatter schemas;
the consumer manifest, pin, defaults, vocabulary, rule levels and templates stay
on their existing paths.

#### Selection data and version boundary

[`SchemaSourceSelection`](../../specht/Manifest/SchemaSourceSelection.cs) is the
two-value input type in `Specht.Manifest`. The existing
[`SpecSchemaSource`](../../specht/Model/SpecSchemaSource.cs) remains the
three-value resolved provenance type in `Model`, so `Manifest` gains no upward
reference. [`SpecStructure`](../../specht/Manifest/SpecStructure.cs) keeps the
nullable manifest selection and whether the original manifest declares an epic
grammar, before omission defaults fill the identifiers.

[`SpecManifestParser`](../../specht/Manifest/SpecManifestParser.cs) accepts
`schemaSource` beginning with shipped schema `0.2.0`. Older pins, including an
omitted pin, reject the key. An unavailable pin with that key is rejected as an
unavailable pin before the parser interprets the selection. The supported input
strings are exactly `embedded` and `disk`; omission stays null so a recorded
`spechtSchema` remains authoritative. The shipped defaults omit the optional
key. No locked resource changes.

#### Resolution and upstream integrity

The parser rejects a manifest that both records an upstream source and declares
`embedded`. A CLI override cannot repair that rejected manifest. Once the
manifest, pin and rule membership are valid,
[`SpecSchemasLoader`](../../specht/Model/SpecSchemasLoader.cs) resolves the CLI
selection ahead of the manifest selection.

| Recorded upstream source | Effective explicit selection | Load and report                                                          |
| ------------------------ | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Absent                   | Absent or `embedded`         | Pinned embedded schemas; `embedded`                                      |
| Absent                   | `disk`                       | Named required files under `.specht/schema/frontmatter/`; `disk`         |
| Present                  | Absent or `disk`             | Recorded local files, verified against their recorded hashes; `upstream` |
| Present                  | `embedded`                   | Contradiction, empty stdout and exit `3` (B-036)                         |

The recorded branch reuses
[`UpstreamSchemaCopy.Verify`](../../specht/Manifest/UpstreamSchemaCopy.cs),
including its existing absent-file and hash-mismatch failures. The explicit
disk path cannot bypass verification. Upstream address/version report fields
remain Report OQ-6's separate scope.

#### Engine and command path

[`CheckCommand`](../Features/Check/CheckCommand.cs) lets Spectre parse and validate
`--schema-source`, maps the two accepted strings to the input enum, and dispatches
the nullable value through its injected delegate. [`Program.cs`](../Program.cs)
registers the runner's overload that accepts the selection. The runner, model
loader and schema loader carry the value per invocation; their root-only
entry points retain automatic selection. No mutable selection is captured by a
singleton and the CLI reads no manifest or schema file.

All local schema reads use
[`SpecManifestStore.ReadFrontmatterSchema`](../../specht/Manifest/SpecManifestStore.cs).
The selected disk branch reads and compiles `feature`, `task`, and then `epic`
only when the original manifest declares that grammar. It honors the validated
`frontmatterSchemas` file names, decodes UTF-8 with a BOM removed, and stops at
the first read or compilation fault. An unselected epic slot retains the pinned
schema. The immutable schemas keep the selected version's number, defaults and
rule vocabulary, with only the selected frontmatter texts replaced.

The loader translates local read and JSON Schema compilation failures into
`SpechtManifestException`, naming `.specht/schema/frontmatter/<name>` with `/`
separators and no underlying exception text. The command's existing fold produces
stderr, empty stdout and exit `3`. JsonSchema.Net defers unresolved reference
failures until a schema is used. The existing evaluation boundary in
[`FrontmatterSchemaRule`](../../specht/Rules/FrontmatterSchemaRule.cs) catches
`RefResolutionException` for local schemas and translates it with the named
schema's relative path. This preserves valid dynamic references and the existing
upfront read/syntax failure order without fetching or creating a separate
reference resolver. `LoadOnDisk` delegates to the same validated
disk selection path. The obsolete direct file-reading overload on `SpecSchemas`
is removed; its compiled-value constructor receives the already compiled schemas.

The accepted ADR-0010 model-reader stage must preserve this per-run input and
policy when it replaces the retained loaders. This change adds no mediator.
Manifest B-008's acceptance binding now selects disk through the model loader;
Manifest OQ-2(b) records the controlled failure policy. Out of scope are
`upgrade`, template ownership, pin policies, new rule ids, upstream fetching and
broader epic opt-in rules.

### Version catalog after ADR-0010 D1

**Implemented 2026-10-13.** The following migration map
replaces the older wiring described below; historical names remain attached to
their original implementation accounts. Manifest § 7 owns the shared sequencing
and failure-preservation design.

| Existing entry point                               | D1 replacement                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SchemaVersions.Embedded`, `ReadEmbedded`          | `Specht.Resources.SpechtResources` reads raw resources; an instance catalog assembles versions in memory |
| `SchemaVersions.Select`                            | Exact, nullable lookup on the catalog; no manifest exception or path dependency                          |
| `SpecManifest.Load` pin/upstream parsing           | Pure `SpecManifestParser` over the resource default and injected catalog                                 |
| `SpecSchemas.Pinned`                               | Parser pin-resolution operation, after text/shape checks                                                 |
| `SpecSchemas.Load` manifest read                   | `SpecSchemasLoader` sequences the store/parser; the retained schema methods receive parsed values        |
| `Run(root, versions)` and loader per-call catalogs | One chosen catalog in the constructed engine, including synthetic catalogs in tests                      |
| `FrontmatterSchemaStore`                           | Local-byte read on `SpecManifestStore`                                                                   |

Keep folder-derived versions, exact equality, numeric enumeration order, schema
texts, rule pages, vocabulary and all failure messages. The catalog's constructors
accept only in-memory resources or version values and reference no other engine
namespace. The parser owns a failed lookup's manifest-relative diagnostic and
rule-membership diagnostic. Unknown keys and shape/level failures still precede
lookup. The host's existing catches retain exits 2 and 3 and empty stdout.

The local upstream store is folded as the owner already required on
2026-10-12, recorded below under the local upstream copy. Verification still compares unchanged original bytes, reports
all absent/mismatched files in the same order, and never fetches during a check.
The fifth-store exception described below ends with D1. The verifier and schema
compiler split are otherwise untouched.

The live-copy default remains the omission source through this refactor.
Manifest decision 0012 supplies versioned omission defaults afterwards; neither
a shipped manifest nor a version-lock digest changes here.

<!-- last written by: implementer, 2026-10-28 (item `0290`) -->

**A version number is three integers (item `0120`, decision 0005).** [`SemanticVersion`](../../specht/Versioning/SemanticVersion.cs) is a value of major, minor and patch. It compares by major, then minor, then patch, each as a number, so `0.10.0` follows `0.9.0`; two versions are equal when all three are; it writes itself as `major.minor.patch` in the invariant culture. `TryParse` accepts text that is exactly three parts separated by dots, each part a non-negative decimal integer, and nothing else. The engine declares the type itself. A versioning package would be a dependency outside the five `0001-F1` C-7 names, for one comparison and one parse; `System.Version` reads `0.1` and `0.1.0.0` as versions, which B-039 rejects.

**What `TryParse` does with input no claim names.** These are design choices, not claims (§ 8 lists them as untested), and each is the narrowest reading of "three numbers":

| Input                                                   | Read as       | Why                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A prerelease or build suffix, `0.2.0-beta.1`, `0.2.0+5` | not a version | The last part is not a number. No special handling and no message of its own; OQ-15 decides whether it becomes one. |
| A part with a leading zero, `0.01.0`                    | not a version | A part must be the text its number writes, so one version has one spelling and a pin written back is the pin read.  |
| A sign, `+0.1.0`, `0.-1.0`                              | not a version | A part is digits only.                                                                                              |
| Whitespace around the text or a part                    | not a version | Nothing is trimmed.                                                                                                 |
| A part too large for a 32-bit integer                   | not a version | The part does not parse.                                                                                            |
| An empty part, `0..1`, or digits outside ASCII          | not a version | The part does not parse.                                                                                            |

**A version is data (item `0042`).** [`SchemaVersion`](../../specht/Versioning/SchemaVersion.cs) is one shipped version: its `Number`, a `SemanticVersion` from item `0120`; the text of its three frontmatter schemas; and the rule ids its vocabulary holds. [`SchemaVersions`](../../specht/Versioning/SchemaVersions.cs) is a set of them, ascending by `Number`. `Select(version)` returns the member whose number equals `version` or throws [`SpechtManifestException`](../../specht/Manifest/SpechtManifestException.cs) with the manifest's repository-relative path, the pinned version and every shipped version, each as `major.minor.patch` (B-003) - the same exception a rejected manifest key throws, so `CheckCommand`'s existing catch already writes it to stderr, leaves stdout empty and exits `3`; no command changed. The set takes its versions by constructor, so a test builds `0.2.0` or `1.0.0` in memory from the embedded `0.1.0` rather than shipping a second schema file. The rule vocabulary is known for `0.1.0` and `0.2.0` only; an embedded folder of any other version is refused at composition, so `0.1.1`'s arm left with its folders ([decision 0013](decisions/0013-schema-0-1-1-stops-shipping.md)).

**A pin is exact (B-055).** `Select` compares for equality and nothing else: over `0.1.0`, `0.2.0` and `1.0.0`, a pin of `0.1.0` is `0.1.0`. No range, no "newest patch", no fallback is read anywhere; a pin policy is item `0124`'s, and it will be a second input to the selection, not a change to what equality means.

**The embedded set (B-004).** `SchemaVersions.Embedded` reads the engine's manifest resources named `schema/v<major.minor.patch>/frontmatter/<file>`, one version per distinct version segment, and fills each version's slots from `feature.schema.json`, `task.schema.json` and `epic.schema.json`. The resources come from the tool's shipping copy (`0001-F4` C-2, item `0051`): `src/specht/specht.csproj` includes `..\tool\schema\v*\frontmatter\*.schema.json` as `EmbeddedResource` under the logical name `schema/v<major.minor.patch>/frontmatter/<file>`, the scheme `src/tool/tool.csproj` already uses.
